*=$0a00

; ---- options, highscore saving and hotkeys ----
; patches the game by cheat_bits, cleared bits switch a cheat on:
; bit 0: men don't run out, bit 1: bonus doesn't count down, bit 2: air doesn't run out,
; bit 3: deadly chars and sprites don't kill, bit 4: C= skips a level, bit 5: B toggles all switches
apply_options:
		lda #$00
		sta highscores_changed
		lda #$00
		sta cheats_on
		lda cheat_bits
		cmp #$ff
		beq l0A16
		lda #$01
		sta cheats_on
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A16:
		lda #$01
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A22
		lda #$ad
		sta decrement_men
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A22:
		lda #$02
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A2E
		lda #$bd
		sta decrement_bonus
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A2E:
		lda #$04
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A3A
		lda #$bd
		sta decrement_air
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A3A:
		lda #$08
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A5D
		lda #$4c
		sta deadly_check
		lda #$ae
		sta deadly_check + 1
		lda #$43
		sta d43A5
		lda #$ea
		sta d4BB3
		sta d4BB3 + 1
		lda #$60
		sta lose_life
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A5D:
		lda #$10
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A67
		inc level_skip_enabled
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A67:
		lda #$20
		bit cheat_bits
		bne l0A71
		inc switch_cheat_enabled
; jumped to by: apply_options
l0A71:
		rts 

; saves the highscore table (part of highscore_screen) with the KERNAL, after a new entry,
; unless cheats were on, then back to the title
; jumped to by: store_highscore_screen
save_highscores:
		sei 
		lda highscores_changed
		cmp #$00
		beq l0AE5
		lda cheats_on
		cmp #$00
		bne l0AE5
		lda #$00
		sta highscores_changed
		lda #>kernal_irq
		sta irq_vector + $1
		lda #<kernal_irq
		sta irq_vector
		lda #$37
		sta cpu_port
		lda #$00
		sta $9d
		lda #$00
		sta sid_vol_filter
		lda #$0f
		tay 
		ldx $ba
		cpx #$08
		bpl l0AA8
		ldx #$08
; jumped to by: save_highscores
l0AA8:
		jsr setlfs
		lda #$0d
		ldx #$60
		ldy #$68
		jsr setnam
		jsr open
		lda #$0f
		jsr close
		ldy #$00
		tya 
		ldx $ba
		cpx #$08
		bpl l0AC7
		ldx #$08
; jumped to by: save_highscores
l0AC7:
		jsr setlfs
		lda #$0b
		ldx #$62
		ldy #$68
		jsr setnam
		ldx #$67
		ldy #$15
		stx $c1
		sty $c2
		lda #$c1
		ldx #$5f
		ldy #$17
		jsr save
		cli 
; jumped to by: save_highscores
l0AE5:
		lda #$00
		sta highscores_changed
		jmp title

; called by: enter_highscore
enter_highscore_name:
		lda #$01
		sta highscores_changed
		lda #$1c
		sta screen_ram + $301
		stx saved_a
		lda screen_ram + $3a6
		sta saved_rank_digit
		ldx #$17
; jumped to by: enter_highscore_name
l0B02:
		lda name_prompt, x
		sta screen_ram + $39f, x
		dex 
		bpl l0B02
		lda saved_rank_digit
		sta screen_ram + $3a6
		jsr name_entry
		rts 

; read by: enter_highscore_name
; written by: enter_highscore_name
saved_rank_digit:
		.byte	$31

; per pass: the cheat keys, R to restart the level, then the objects
; called by: main_loop
check_keys:
		lda level_skip_enabled
		beq l0B1E
		jsr check_level_skip_key
; jumped to by: check_keys
l0B1E:
		lda switch_cheat_enabled
		beq l0B26
		jsr check_switch_cheat_key
; jumped to by: check_keys
l0B26:
		jsr check_restart_key
		jsr update_objects
		rts 

; called by: check_keys
check_restart_key:
		sta saved_a
		lda cia1_port_a
		sta saved_port_a
		lda #$fb
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda cia1_port_b
		cmp #$fd
		beq l0B4C
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda saved_a
		rts 

; jumped to by: check_restart_key
l0B4C:
		lda current_player
		cmp #$02
		bne l0B5B
		lda player_levels + $1
		sta level
		jmp l0B60

; jumped to by: check_restart_key
l0B5B:
		lda player_levels
		sta level
; jumped to by: check_restart_key
l0B60:
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda saved_a
		jmp start_level

; called by: check_keys
check_switch_cheat_key:
		lda #$40
		cmp last_key
		beq l0B73
		rts 

; jumped to by: check_switch_cheat_key
l0B73:
		lda cia1_port_a
		sta saved_port_a
		lda #$f7
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda cia1_port_b
		cmp #$ef
		beq l0B92
		lda cia1_port_a
		sta d6875
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		rts 

; jumped to by: check_switch_cheat_key
l0B92:
		lda switches + $1
		cmp #$01
		bne l0BA6
		lda #$bd
		sta d4753
		sta d4764
		lda #$00
		jmp l0BB0

; jumped to by: check_switch_cheat_key
l0BA6:
		lda #$9d
		sta d4753
		sta d4764
		lda #$01
; jumped to by: check_switch_cheat_key
l0BB0:
		sta switches
		sta switches + $1
		sta switches + $2
		sta switches + $3
		sta switches + $4
		sta switches + $5
		sta switches + $6
		sta switches + $7
		sta switches + $8
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		jsr wait_raster
		rts 

; called by: check_keys
check_level_skip_key:
		lda #$7f
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda cia1_port_b
		cmp #$df
		beq l0BE2
		rts 

; jumped to by: check_level_skip_key
l0BE2:
		lda current_player
		cmp #$01
		bne l0C02
		lda player_levels
		cmp #$12
		bne l0BFA
		lda #$01
		sta level
		sta player_levels
		jmp start_level

; jumped to by: check_level_skip_key
l0BFA:
		inc player_levels
		lda player_levels
		sta level
; jumped to by: check_level_skip_key
l0C02:
		lda current_player
		cmp #$02
		bne l0C22
		lda player_levels + $1
		cmp #$12
		bne l0C1A
		lda #$01
		sta level
		sta player_levels + $1
		jmp start_level

; jumped to by: check_level_skip_key
l0C1A:
		inc player_levels + $1
		lda player_levels + $1
		sta level
; jumped to by: check_level_skip_key
l0C22:
		jmp start_level

; draws the level: the border char and colour, then the level stream from screen $0429:
; $fe ends it and starts the enemy list, 0 n skips n cells, 1 a conveyor, 2 a bridge,
; 3 a force beam, 4 a force column, 5 a lift, 6 an escalator, 7 a rope, 9 a piston,
; 10 a ladder, else a run of (char, colour, count)
; called by: setup_level
draw_level:
		jsr clear_screen
		ldy #$00
		jsr find_level_and_read
		lda (level_pointer_lo), y
		sta temp_1
		jsr next_level_byte
		sta border_color
		sta temp_2
		ldx #$00

		sta screen_pointer_hi
		lda #$00
		sta screen_pointer_lo
		tay

		sta vic_sprite_coords + $4, y

		ora vic_sprite_x_high_bits
		sta vic_sprite_x_high_bits
		inx 
		jsr sprite_bit_x
		ora vic_sprite_enable
		sta vic_sprite_enable
		dex 
		lda #$01
		sta enemy_d, x
		lda #$03
		sta enemy_f, x
		rts 

; called by: read_enemy_position, setup_enemies

		jsr next_level_byte
		ldy color_pointer_hi
		rts

		jsr sprite_bit_y
		eor vic_sprite_x_high_bits
		sta vic_sprite_x_high_bits

		jsr next_enemy_byte
		sta vic_sprite_coords + $3, x
		rts

; the player moves, then the char under the feet acts: $24 slows, $25 and $2b kill,
; $27 starts to crumble ($44-$4a, then gone), $28 bounces, $2f is sticky;
; at the body $3a, $3d, $3e and $4e-$52 kill, $3f/$40 (escalator) carry up,
; $59-$5c are switches, $5e is the exit, $61-$63 are items worth 100;
; off a ladder ($57/$58), rope ($43) or escalator the player falls
; called by: main_loop

		dec move_countdown
		lda move_countdown
		cmp #$00
		beq l437C
		rts

		jmp wait_key_and_save

; ---- main flow ----
; jumped to by: save_highscores, read_input
title:
		lda #$07
		sta vic_sprite_colors
		lda #$06
		sta sprite_multicolor_1
		lda #$08
		sta sprite_multicolor_2
		lda #$18
		sta vic_memory_setup
		lda #$ff
		jsr play_title_music
; jumped to by: title
l4A99:
		jsr read_input
		nop 
		bne l4A99
; jumped to by: run_demo
l4A9F:
		jsr show_title_screen
		lda #$00
		sta jiffy_clock + $1
; jumped to by: after_read_input
l4AA6:
		lda jiffy_clock + $1
		cmp #$08
		beq run_demo
		jsr read_input
; where the IRQ interrupted the main code (the KERNAL's $ff48 pushed A, X and Y, the handler did a pha)
after_read_input:
		nop 
		bne select_players
		jmp l4AA6

; jumped to by: title
run_demo:
		jsr demo
; jumped to by: set_music
l4AB8:
		lda #$00
		sta jiffy_clock + $1
; jumped to by: run_demo
l4ABC:
		lda jiffy_clock + $1
		cmp #$08
		beq l4A9F
		jsr read_input
		nop 
		beq l4ABC
; jumped to by: after_read_input
select_players:
		jsr read_input
		nop 
		bne select_players
		jsr wait_no_key
		ldx #$00
		lda #$04
		sta background_color
		sta border_color
; jumped to by: select_players
l4ADB:
		lda control_text, x
		sta screen_ram + $149, x
		lda players_text, x
		sta screen_ram + $d1, x
		lda #$00
		sta color_ram + $149, x
		sta color_ram + $d1, x
		inx 
		cpx #$14
		bne l4ADB
		sta keyboard_control
		lda #$01
		sta color_ram + $149
		sta color_ram + $dc
		sta players_left
		lda #$c8
		sta vic_control_2
; jumped to by: select_players
l4B06:
		jsr show_start_messages
		nop 
		bne l4B06
; jumped to by: after_wait_choice
l4B0C:
		jsr wait_choice
; the return address under it on the stack
after_wait_choice:
		nop 
		beq start_game
; jumped to by: wait_choice
l4B12:
		cmp #$38
		bne l4B26
		lda #$00
		sta color_ram + $e1
		lda #$01
		sta color_ram + $dc
		sta players_left
		jmp l4B0C

; jumped to by: after_wait_choice
l4B26:
		cmp #$3b
		bne l4B3C
		lda #$00
		sta color_ram + $dc
		lda #$01
		sta color_ram + $e1
		lda #$03
		sta players_left
		jmp l4B0C

; jumped to by: after_wait_choice
l4B3C:
		cmp #$22
		bne l4B4F
		lda #$00
		sta color_ram + $155
		sta keyboard_control
		lda #$01
		sta color_ram + $149
		jmp l4B0C

; jumped to by: after_wait_choice
l4B4F:
		cmp #$25
		bne l4B0C
		lda #$01
		sta color_ram + $155
		sta keyboard_control
		lda #$00
		sta color_ram + $149
		jmp l4B0C

; jumped to by: after_wait_choice, wait_choice
start_game:
		lda #$01
		sta current_player
		sta player_levels
		sta player_levels + $1
		sta level
		lda #$00
		sta background_color
		jsr init_status_lines
; jumped to by: check_restart_key, check_level_skip_key, next_player, touch_exit
start_level:
		jsr prepare_level
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		jsr setup_level
		jsr show_player_and_status
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		lda vic_sprite_collision
; jumped to by: d4BB3
main_loop:
		jsr music_keys
		jsr update_player
		jsr check_keys
		jsr update_enemies
		jsr animate_charset
		jsr count_air_and_bonus
		lda vic_sprite_collision
		and #$01
		cmp #$01
; written by: apply_options
d4BB3:
		beq l4BB8
		jmp main_loop

; jumped to by: d4BB3
l4BB8:
		jsr lose_life
; the demo: shows levels 1-9 for a moment each
; called by: run_demo
demo:
		lda #$01
		sta player_levels
		lda #$00
		sta background_color
		lda #$c8
		sta vic_control_2
; jumped to by: demo
l4BCA:
		lda player_levels
		sta level
		jsr setup_level
		lda #$00
		sta screen_pointer_lo
; jumped to by: demo
l4BD6:
		ldy #$00
		jsr delay
		jsr read_input
		nop 
		bne l4BF3
		inc screen_pointer_lo
		lda screen_pointer_lo
		cmp #$07
		bne l4BD6
		inc player_levels
		lda player_levels
		cmp #$0a
		bne l4BCA
; jumped to by: demo
l4BF3:
		lda #$00
		sta vic_sprite_enable
		jsr show_highscore_screen
		rts 

; jumped to by: check_highscores
wait_key_and_save:
		lda last_key
		cmp #$40
		bne wait_key_and_save
		ldy #$00
		jsr delay
		lda #$00
		sta jiffy_clock + $1
		jmp store_highscore_screen

; jumped to by: d4764
switch_on_sound:
		lda #$64
		sta voice2_frequency + $1
		lda #$00
		sta voice2_control
		lda #$11
		sta voice2_control
		rts 

; jumped to by: d4753
switch_off_sound:
		lda #$0a
		sta voice2_frequency + $1
		lda #$00
		sta voice2_control
		lda #$21
		sta voice2_control
		rts 

; called by: close_level_wipe
wipe_sound:
		lda level_pointer_lo
		sta voice2_frequency + $1
		lda #$00
		sta voice2_control
		lda #$21
		sta voice2_control
		jsr next_screen_row
		rts 

; every 10 passes: air one down (dying at 0), and the bonus one down
; called by: main_loop
count_air_and_bonus:
		dec air_countdown
		lda air_countdown
		cmp #$00
		beq l4C4C
		rts 

; jumped to by: count_air_and_bonus
l4C4C:
		ldy #$03
; jumped to by: count_air_and_bonus
l4C4E:
		lda screen_ram + $3d9, y
		cmp #$30
		bne l4C5D
		dey 
		cpy #$ff
		bne l4C4E
		jmp lose_life

; jumped to by: count_air_and_bonus
l4C5D:
		ldx #$03
; written by: apply_options
decrement_air:
		dec screen_ram + $3d9, x
		lda screen_ram + $3d9, x
		cmp #$2f
		beq l4C6C
		jmp l4C75

; jumped to by: decrement_air
l4C6C:
		lda #$39
		sta screen_ram + $3d9, x
		dex 
		jmp decrement_air

; jumped to by: decrement_air
l4C75:
		lda #$0a
		sta air_countdown
		ldx #$03
; written by: apply_options
decrement_bonus:
		dec screen_ram + $3e4, x
		lda screen_ram + $3e4, x
		cmp #$2f
		beq l4C89
		rts 

		nop 
		nop 
; jumped to by: decrement_bonus
l4C89:
		lda #$39
		sta screen_ram + $3e4, x
		dex 
		jmp decrement_bonus

		rts 

; called by: touch_exit
wrap_level:
		lda #$01
		sta animation_countdown, x
		rts 

		rts 
		.byte	$00

sid_init:
		txa 
; jumped to by: sid_init
l4C9C:
		sta voice1_frequency + $1, x
		inx 
		cpx #$18
		bne l4C9C
		lda #$0f
		sta sid_vol_filter
		lda #$88
		sta voice1_attack_decay
		lda #$09
		sta voice1_sustain_release
		rts 

; jumped to by: wait_key_and_save
store_highscore_screen:
		ldx #$00
; jumped to by: store_highscore_screen
l4CB6:
		lda screen_ram, x
		sta highscore_screen, x
		lda screen_ram + $fa, x
		sta highscore_screen + $fa, x
		lda screen_ram + $1f4, x
		sta highscore_screen + $1f4, x
		lda screen_ram + $2ee, x
		sta highscore_screen + $2ee, x
		inx 
		cpx #$fa
		bne l4CB6
		jmp save_highscores

; called by: title, set_music
play_title_music:
		jsr set_music
		jsr music_silence_1_3
		lda #$10
		sta sid_registers + $4
		sta sid_registers + $12
		lda #$88
		sta sid_registers + $c
		lda #$09
		sta sid_registers + $d
		jsr init_sid
		lda #$00
		sta voice_1_page
		sta voice_1_position
		sta voice_3_position
		sta voice_3_page
		lda #$03
		sta voice_speeds
		sta voice_speeds + $2
		jsr music_play_1_3
		rts 

; called by: prepare_level
play_game_music:
		jsr music_silence_1_3
		jsr install_irq
		lda #$40
		sta sid_registers + $4
		sta sid_registers + $12
		jsr init_sid
		lda #$00
		sta voice_1_page
		sta voice_1_position
		sta voice_3_position
		sta voice_3_page
		lda #$03
		sta voice_speeds
		sta voice_speeds + $2
		jsr music_play_1_3
		rts 

; called by: play_title_music
set_music:
		sta vic_sprite_multicolor
		jsr install_irq
		rts 

		lda #$ff
		jsr play_title_music
		jmp l4AB8

; F5 turns the music off, F7 on
; called by: main_loop
music_keys:
		lda last_key
		cmp #$06
		bne l4D51
		lda #$00
		sta sid_registers + $18
		rts 

; jumped to by: music_keys
l4D51:
		cmp #$03
		bne l4D5A
		lda #$0f
		sta sid_registers + $18
; jumped to by: music_keys
l4D5A:
		rts 

; jumped to by: update_force_beam
clear_force_beam:
		lda object_a, x
		tay 
		dey 
; jumped to by: clear_force_beam
l4D60:
		lda (screen_pointer_lo), y
		cmp #$2e
		bne l4D6A
		lda #$20
		sta (screen_pointer_lo), y
; jumped to by: clear_force_beam
l4D6A:
		dey 
		cpy #$ff
		bne l4D60
		rts 

; jumped to by: update_force_beam
draw_force_beam:
		lda object_a, x
		tay 
		dey 
; jumped to by: draw_force_beam
l4D75:
		lda (screen_pointer_lo), y
		cmp #$20
		bne l4D7F
		lda #$2e
		sta (screen_pointer_lo), y
; jumped to by: draw_force_beam
l4D7F:
		dey 
		cpy #$ff
		bne l4D75
		rts

; jumped to by: name_entry
name_entry_keys:
		lda #$00
		sta screen_pointer_lo
		lda #$0b
		sta name_column
; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l66A9:
		jsr getin
		bne l66B8
		ldx screen_pointer_lo
		lda #$26
		sta screen_ram + $303, x
		jmp l66A9

; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l66B8:
		clc 
		cmp #$a0
		bne l66C2
		lda #$20
		jmp l6707

; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l66C2:
		cmp #$20
		beq l6707
		cmp #$14
		bne l66E7
		lda screen_pointer_lo
		cmp #$00
		beq l66E7
		dec screen_pointer_lo
		lda #$20
		jsr chrout
		ldx #$13
		dec name_column
		ldy name_column
		jsr plot
		ldx screen_pointer_lo
		jmp l66A9

; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l66E7:
		cmp #$0d
		bne l66FE
		lda #$20
		jsr chrout
		lda #$35
		sta screen_ram + $301
		jsr show_top5_text
		jsr wait_raster
		jmp sort_highscores

; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l66FE:
		cmp #$41
		bcc l66A9
		clc 
		cmp #$5b
		bcs l66A9
; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l6707:
		jsr chrout
		inc screen_pointer_lo
		inc name_column
		lda screen_pointer_lo
		cmp #$0a
		bne l66A9
		lda #$35
		sta screen_ram + $301
		jsr show_top5_text
		jsr wait_raster
		rts 

		ldx #$00
		lda #$20
; jumped to by: name_entry_keys
l6725:
		sta screen_ram + $303, x
		inx 
		cpx #$0a
		bne l6725
		lda #$35
		sta screen_ram + $301
		jsr show_top5_text
		jsr wait_raster
		jmp sort_highscores

; called by: check_switch_cheat_key, name_entry_keys, read_input, wait_choice
wait_raster:
		cmp vic_raster
		bne wait_raster
		rts 

; called by: name_entry_keys
show_top5_text:
		stx saved_a
		ldx #$17
; jumped to by: show_top5_text
l6746:
		lda top5_text, x
		sta screen_ram + $39f, x
		dex 
		bpl l6746
		rts 

; returns 1 when fire or space is pressed; H shows the highscores
; called by: name_entry, title, run_demo, select_players, demo, show_start_messages
read_input:
		lda cia1_port_a
		sta saved_port_a
		lda #$f7
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda cia1_port_b
		sta last_port_b
		cmp #$df
		beq l6771
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda saved_a
		jmp l6784

; jumped to by: read_input
l6771:
		jsr show_highscore_screen
		ldx #$f0
; jumped to by: read_input
l6776:
		jsr wait_raster
		nop 
		nop 
		jsr wait_raster
		dex 
		bne l6776
		jmp title

; jumped to by: read_input
l6784:
		lda saved_port_a
		sta cia1_port_a
		lda cia1_port_a
		and #$10
		beq l679D
		lda cia1_port_b
		cmp #$ef
		beq l679D
		lda #$00
		jmp l679F

; jumped to by: read_input
l679D:
		lda #$01
; jumped to by: read_input
l679F:
		rts 

; called by: select_players
wait_choice:
		sta saved_a
; jumped to by: wait_choice
l67A3:
		lda cia1_port_a
		cmp #$7f
		bne l67A3
		jsr wait_raster
		lda cia1_port_a
		and #$10
		beq l67C6
		lda last_key
		cmp #$3c
		beq l67C6
		lda last_key
		cmp #$01
		rts 

		beq l67C6
		lda last_key
		jmp l4B12

; jumped to by: wait_choice
l67C6:
		lda saved_a
		jmp start_game

; called by: select_players
show_start_messages:
		ldx #$27
; jumped to by: show_start_messages
l67CE:
		lda continue_message, x
		sta screen_ram + $208, x
		lda restart_message, x
		sta screen_ram + $258, x
		lda #$0f
		sta color_ram + $208, x
		lda #$07
		sta color_ram + $258, x
		dex 
		bpl l67CE
		jsr read_input
		rts 

; jumped to by: decrement_men
stop_sound_game_over:
		lda #$00
		sta sid_vol_filter
		jmp game_over

; called by: select_players
wait_no_key:
		sta saved_a
; jumped to by: wait_no_key
l67F6:
		lda cia1_port_b
		cmp #$ff
		bne l67F6
		lda saved_a
		jsr clear_screen
		rts 

; called by: start_level
prepare_level:
		lda #$9d
		sta d4753
		sta d4764
		jsr play_game_music
		rts 

; read by: show_start_messages
continue_message:
		.text	"    PRESS FIRE OR SPACE TO CONTI"
		.text	"NUE     "
; read by: show_start_messages
restart_message:
		.text	" TO RESTART LEVEL PLEASE PRESS R"
		.text	" INGAME "

highscore_filename:
		.enc	"none"
		.text	"S:HIGHSCOREBM"

; read by: name_entry_keys
; written by: name_entry_keys
; modified by: name_entry_keys
name_column:
		.byte	$0f
; set when the table got a new name
; read by: save_highscores
; written by: apply_options, save_highscores, enter_highscore_name
highscores_changed:		.byte	$00
; set when cheats are on: the highscores aren't saved
; read by: save_highscores
; written by: apply_options
cheats_on:		.byte	$00
; read by: check_keys
; modified by: apply_options
level_skip_enabled:		.byte	$00
; read by: check_keys
; modified by: apply_options
switch_cheat_enabled:		.byte	$00
; read by: check_restart_key, read_input, wait_choice, wait_no_key
; written by: enter_highscore_name, check_restart_key, show_top5_text, wait_choice, wait_no_key
saved_a:		.byte	$01
; read by: check_restart_key, check_switch_cheat_key, read_input
; written by: check_restart_key, check_switch_cheat_key, read_input
saved_port_a:		.byte	$7f
; written by: read_input
last_port_b:		.byte	$ff
; written by: check_switch_cheat_key
d6875:		.byte	$00

; read by: enter_highscore_name
name_prompt:
		.enc	"screen"
		.text	"PLAYER   INPUT YOUR NAME"
; read by: show_top5_text
top5_text:
		.text	"    THE TOP 5 HEROES    "

.virtual $68a6
unused_68a6:
		.fill	680
.endv

*=$7000

; ---- music ----
; The music player plays up to three voices, each with its own copy of the player code
; (voice_1_play at $c100, voice_2_play at $c200, voice_3_play at $c300) and its own two
; parallel streams: a note stream and, $800 further, a parameter stream, read at the same index.
; Voice 1 reads $e000/$e800 (under the KERNAL ROM, copied there from $9000/$9800), voice 2
; $a000/$a800 (empty, the game never plays voice 2), voice 3 $b000/$b800.
; A note byte with its parameter means:
; - $00: return from a call, or stop the voice when not in one;
; - $ff with 0: call: the next pair holds the position (note stream) and page (parameter stream)
;   to go to; the return position goes to the voice's call slot (one level only);
; - $ff with n: repeat: jump to the next pair's position n times, then go on after it;
; - $7f with d: a rest of 2*d ticks;
; - else a note number with d: plays the note (note_freq_hi/lo) for 2*d ticks, and releases the
;   gate one tick early unless d has bit 7 set.
; A tick is voice_speeds+1 IRQs. The game plays one tune at position 0 on voices 1 and 3, with
; triangle waves on the title (play_title_music) and pulse waves in the game (play_game_music).
; a copy of the music player, and the loader at $7800

		.byte	$1c, $1f, $22, $10, $1a, $1c, $1f, $0c, $1c, $1f, $22, $0a, $13, $16, $19, $07
		.byte	$13, $16, $1a, $1c, $7f, $7f, $7f, $7f, $7f, $25, $22, $1f, $1c, $22, $16, $1c
		.byte	$11, $7f, $0a, $0d, $10, $13, $16, $19, $1c, $1f, $0a, $13, $1f, $13, $1f, $13
		.byte	$1f, $13, $1c, $13, $1c, $13, $1c, $13, $1f, $13, $1f, $13, $1f, $13, $22, $13
		.byte	$22, $13, $22, $13, $ff, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
		.byte	$00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $1f

.virtual $b217
unused_b217:
		.fill	1481
; read by: voice_3_tick
; written by: voice_3_tick, voice_3_silence
voice_3_in_call:		.byte	?
; read by: voice_3_tick
; written by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_return_page:		.byte	?
; read by: voice_3_tick
; written by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_return_position:
		.fill	30
.endv

*=$b800

		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02
		.byte	$02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $01, $01, $01

		.byte	$01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $84, $01, $01, $81, $81, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $81, $81, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$81, $81, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $84, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $02, $04, $04, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01, $01
		.byte	$04, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02
		.byte	$02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02, $02
		.byte	$02, $02, $02, $0c, $ff, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
		.byte	$00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $0c

.virtual $ba17
unused_ba17:
		.fill	1490
; unpack_music's parameters, copied from unpack_parameters
unpack_block:
		.fill	22
; read by: move_blocks
; written by: unpack_music
dBFFF:		.byte	?
.endv

*=$c000

; the note frequencies, high and low bytes, by note number
; read by: voice_1_tick, voice_2_tick, voice_3_tick
note_freq_hi:
		.byte	$02, $05, $00, $20, $05, $05, $05, $06, $06, $06, $07, $07, $07, $07, $00, $00
		.byte	$08, $08, $08, $09, $09, $09, $0a, $00, $00, $0b, $0b, $0b, $0c, $0d, $0d, $0e
		.byte	$0e, $0e, $0f, $00, $00, $10, $11, $11, $12, $13, $13, $15, $00, $00, $16, $17
		.byte	$17, $19, $1a, $1a, $1c, $1d, $1d, $1f, $00, $00, $21, $23, $23, $25, $27, $27
		.byte	$2a, $00, $00, $2c, $2f, $2f, $32, $35, $35, $38, $3b, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
; read by: voice_1_tick, voice_2_tick, voice_3_tick
note_freq_lo:
		.byte	$fb, $47, $00, $06, $98, $ed, $ed, $47, $a7, $a7, $0c, $77, $77, $e9, $02, $03
		.byte	$61, $e1, $e1, $68, $f7, $f7, $8f, $05, $06, $30, $da, $da, $8f, $4e, $4e, $18
		.byte	$ef, $ef, $d2, $02, $03, $c3, $c3, $c3, $d1, $ef, $ef, $1f, $05, $06, $60, $b5
		.byte	$b5, $1e, $9c, $9c, $31, $df, $df, $a5, $02, $03, $87, $86, $86, $a2, $df, $df
		.byte	$3e, $05, $06, $c1, $6b, $6b, $3c, $39, $39, $63, $be, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
; copied to the SID ($d401-$d418) by init_sid
; read by: voice_1_tick, voice_2_tick, voice_3_tick, init_sid
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music, music_keys
sid_registers:
		.byte	$00, $00, $02, $09, $10, $26, $22, $00, $00, $00, $0f, $40, $88, $09, $00, $00
		.byte	$00, $0e, $10, $27, $22, $20, $00, $00, $00, $fa, $fc, $fa
; IRQs per tick, minus one, per voice
; read by: voice_1_tick, voice_2_tick, voice_3_tick
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music
voice_speeds:
		.byte	$03, $03, $03, $00
; read by: voice_1_tick
; written by: voice_1_tick, voice_1_silence
; modified by: voice_1_tick
voice_1_duration:		.byte	$01
; read by: voice_1_play
; written by: voice_1_tick, voice_1_silence
; modified by: voice_1_play
voice_1_speed_countdown:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_1_tick
; written by: voice_1_tick, voice_1_silence
; modified by: voice_1_tick
voice_1_repeat_count:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_1_tick, dC1CD, voice_1_read_note, voice_1_read_parameter
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music, voice_1_tick, voice_1_silence
; modified by: voice_1_next
voice_1_position:		.byte	$22
; added to the streams' first page
; read by: voice_1_tick, voice_1_set_page
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music, voice_1_tick, voice_2_tick
; modified by: dC1CD
voice_1_page:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_2_tick
; written by: voice_2_tick, voice_2_silence
; modified by: voice_2_tick
voice_2_duration:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_2_play
; written by: voice_2_tick, voice_2_silence
; modified by: voice_2_play
voice_2_speed_countdown:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_2_tick
; written by: voice_2_tick, voice_2_silence
; modified by: voice_2_tick
voice_2_repeat_count:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_2_tick, dC2CD, voice_2_read_note, voice_2_read_parameter
; written by: voice_2_tick, voice_2_silence
; modified by: voice_2_next
voice_2_position:		.byte	$02
; read by: voice_2_tick, voice_2_set_page
; written by: voice_2_tick
; modified by: dC2CD
voice_2_page:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_3_tick
; written by: voice_3_tick, voice_3_silence
; modified by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_duration:		.byte	$01
; read by: voice_3_play
; written by: voice_3_tick, voice_3_silence
; modified by: voice_3_play
voice_3_speed_countdown:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_3_tick
; written by: voice_3_tick, voice_3_silence
; modified by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_repeat_count:		.byte	$00
; read by: voice_3_tick, dC3CD, voice_3_read_note, voice_3_read_parameter
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music, voice_3_tick, voice_3_silence
; modified by: voice_3_next
voice_3_position:		.byte	$0c
; read by: voice_3_tick, voice_3_set_page
; written by: play_title_music, play_game_music, voice_3_tick
; modified by: dC3CD
voice_3_page:		.byte	$00
; bit 0-2: voice 1-3 plays
; read by: voice_1_silence, voice_2_silence, voice_3_silence, music_play_1, music_play_2, music_play_3, music_play_1_2, music_play_1_3, music_play_2_3, music_play_all, irq
; written by: voice_1_silence, voice_2_silence, voice_3_silence, music_play_1, music_play_2, music_play_3, music_play_1_2, music_play_1_3, music_play_2_3, music_play_all
voices_playing:		.byte	$05, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
		.byte	$00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
		.byte	$00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00
		.byte	$00

; per IRQ: counts the speed down, then the duration, then reads the next note
; called by: snapshot_pc
voice_1_play:
		lda voice_1_speed_countdown
		beq voice_1_tick
		dec voice_1_speed_countdown
		rts 

; called by: start_voice_1
; jumped to by: voice_1_play
voice_1_tick:
		lda voice_speeds
		sta voice_1_speed_countdown
		lda voice_1_duration
		beq lC127
		cmp #$01
		bne lC123
		jsr voice_1_read_parameter
		bmi lC123
		ldx sid_registers + $4
		stx voice1_control
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC123:
		dec voice_1_duration
		rts 

; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC127:
		jsr voice_1_next
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC12A:
		jsr voice_1_read_note
		bne lC14E
		lda voice_1_in_call
		bne lC137
		jmp voice_1_silence

; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC137:
		lda voice_1_return_page
		sta voice_1_page
		jsr voice_1_set_page
		lda voice_1_return_position
		sta voice_1_position
		lda #$00
		sta voice_1_in_call
		clc 
		bcc lC192
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC14E:
		cmp #$ff
		bne lC198
		jsr voice_1_read_parameter
		bne lC168
		lda #$01
		sta voice_1_in_call
		lda voice_1_page
		sta voice_1_return_page
		sty voice_1_return_position
		clc 
		bcc lC175
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC168:
		lda voice_1_repeat_count
		bmi lC16F
		bne lC18D
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC16F:
		jsr voice_1_read_parameter
		sta voice_1_repeat_count
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC175:
		jsr voice_1_next
		jsr voice_1_read_parameter
		sta voice_1_page
		jsr voice_1_read_note
		sta voice_1_position
		jsr voice_1_set_page
		ldy voice_1_position
		clc 
		bcc lC12A
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC18D:
		dec voice_1_repeat_count
		bne lC175
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC192:
		jsr voice_1_next
		clc 
		bcc lC127
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC198:
		cmp #$7f
		beq lC1B0
		tax 
		lda note_freq_hi, x
		sta voice1_frequency + $1
		lda note_freq_lo, x
		sta voice1_frequency
		ldx sid_registers + $4
		inx 
		stx voice1_control
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick
lC1B0:
		jsr voice_1_read_parameter
		asl 
		sta voice_1_duration
		jmp lC123
		.byte	$00

; called by: voice_1_tick, start_voice_1
; jumped to by: dC1CD
voice_1_set_page:
		lda voice_1_page
		clc 
		adc #$e0
		sta $c1dd
		adc #$08
		sta dC1E4
		rts 

; called by: voice_1_tick
; written by: start_voice_1
voice_1_next:
		inc voice_1_position
; written by: start_voice_1
dC1CD:
		ldy voice_1_position
		bne lC1D7
		inc voice_1_page
		bne voice_1_set_page
; jumped to by: dC1CD
lC1D7:
		rts 

; called by: voice_1_tick
voice_1_read_note:
		ldy voice_1_position
		lda voice_1_notes, y
		rts 

; called by: voice_1_tick
voice_1_read_parameter:
		ldy voice_1_position
		lda voice_1_parameters, y
; written by: voice_1_set_page
dC1E4 = $c1e4
		rts 

; also how a voice stops when its stream ends
; jumped to by: voice_1_tick, silence_voice_1
voice_1_silence:
		lda voices_playing
		and #$fe
		sta voices_playing
		lda #$00
		sta voice_1_in_call
		sta voice_1_repeat_count
		sta voice_1_duration
		sta voice_1_speed_countdown
		sta voice_1_position
		rts 

; voice 2's copy of the code; it stores its page in voice_1_page after a call, a bug that
; doesn't show because voice 2 is never played
; called by: snapshot_pc
voice_2_play:
		lda voice_2_speed_countdown
		beq voice_2_tick
		dec voice_2_speed_countdown
		rts 

; called by: start_voice_2
; jumped to by: voice_2_play
voice_2_tick:
		lda voice_speeds + $1
		sta voice_2_speed_countdown
		lda voice_2_duration
		beq lC227
		cmp #$01
		bne lC223
		jsr voice_2_read_parameter
		bmi lC223
		ldx sid_registers + $b
		stx voice2_control
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC223:
		dec voice_2_duration
		rts 

; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC227:
		jsr voice_2_next
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC22A:
		jsr voice_2_read_note
		bne lC24E
		lda voice_2_in_call
		bne lC237
		jmp voice_2_silence

; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC237:
		lda voice_2_return_page
		sta voice_2_page
		jsr voice_2_set_page
		lda voice_2_return_position
		sta voice_2_position
		lda #$00
		sta voice_2_in_call
		clc 
		bcc lC292
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC24E:
		cmp #$ff
		bne lC298
		jsr voice_2_read_parameter
		bne lC268
		lda #$01
		sta voice_2_in_call
		lda voice_2_page
		sta voice_2_return_page
		sty voice_2_return_position
		clc 
		bcc lC275
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC268:
		lda voice_2_repeat_count
		bmi lC26F
		bne lC28D
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC26F:
		jsr voice_2_read_parameter
		sta voice_2_repeat_count
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC275:
		jsr voice_2_next
		jsr voice_2_read_parameter
		sta voice_1_page
		jsr voice_2_read_note
		sta voice_2_position
		jsr voice_2_set_page
		ldy voice_2_position
		clc 
		bcc lC22A
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC28D:
		dec voice_2_repeat_count
		bne lC275
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC292:
		jsr voice_2_next
		clc 
		bcc lC227
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC298:
		cmp #$7f
		beq lC2B0
		tax 
		lda note_freq_hi, x
		sta voice2_frequency + $1
		lda note_freq_lo, x
		sta voice2_frequency
		ldx sid_registers + $b
		inx 
		stx voice2_control
; jumped to by: voice_2_tick
lC2B0:
		jsr voice_2_read_parameter
		asl 
		sta voice_2_duration
		jmp lC223
		.byte	$00

; called by: voice_2_tick, start_voice_2
; jumped to by: dC2CD
voice_2_set_page:
		lda voice_2_page
		clc 
		adc #$a0
		sta $c2dd
		adc #$08
		sta dC2E4
		rts 

; called by: voice_2_tick
; written by: start_voice_2
voice_2_next:
		inc voice_2_position
; written by: start_voice_2
dC2CD:
		ldy voice_2_position
		bne lC2D7
		inc voice_2_page
		bne voice_2_set_page
; jumped to by: dC2CD
lC2D7:
		rts 

; called by: voice_2_tick
voice_2_read_note:
		ldy voice_2_position
		lda voice_2_notes, y
		rts 

; called by: voice_2_tick
voice_2_read_parameter:
		ldy voice_2_position
		lda voice_2_parameters, y
; written by: voice_2_set_page
dC2E4 = $c2e4
		rts 

; jumped to by: voice_2_tick, silence_voice_2
voice_2_silence:
		lda voices_playing
		and #$fd
		sta voices_playing
		lda #$00
		sta voice_2_in_call
		sta voice_2_repeat_count
		sta voice_2_duration
		sta voice_2_speed_countdown
		sta voice_2_position
		rts 

; called by: snapshot_pc
voice_3_play:
		lda voice_3_speed_countdown
		beq voice_3_tick
		dec voice_3_speed_countdown
		rts 

; called by: start_voice_3
; jumped to by: voice_3_play
voice_3_tick:
		lda voice_speeds + $2
		sta voice_3_speed_countdown
		lda voice_3_duration
		beq lC327
		cmp #$01
		bne lC323
		jsr voice_3_read_parameter
		bmi lC323
		ldx sid_registers + $12
		stx voice3_control
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC323:
		dec voice_3_duration
		rts 

; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC327:
		jsr voice_3_next
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC32A:
		jsr voice_3_read_note
		bne lC34E
		lda voice_3_in_call
		bne lC337
		jmp voice_3_silence

; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC337:
		lda voice_3_return_page
		sta voice_3_page
		jsr voice_3_set_page
		lda voice_3_return_position
		sta voice_3_position
		lda #$00
		sta voice_3_in_call
		clc 
		bcc lC392
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC34E:
		cmp #$ff
		bne lC398
		jsr voice_3_read_parameter
		bne lC368
		lda #$01
		sta voice_3_in_call
		lda voice_3_page
		sta voice_3_return_page
		sty voice_3_return_position
		clc 
		bcc lC375
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC368:
		lda voice_3_repeat_count
		bmi lC36F
		bne lC38D
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC36F:
		jsr voice_3_read_parameter
		sta voice_3_repeat_count
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC375:
		jsr voice_3_next
		jsr voice_3_read_parameter
		sta voice_3_page
		jsr voice_3_read_note
		sta voice_3_position
		jsr voice_3_set_page
		ldy voice_3_position
		clc 
		bcc lC32A
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC38D:
		dec voice_3_repeat_count
		bne lC375
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC392:
		jsr voice_3_next
		clc 
		bcc lC327
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC398:
		cmp #$7f
		beq lC3B0
		tax 
		lda note_freq_hi, x
		sta voice3_frequency + $1
		lda note_freq_lo, x
		sta voice3_frequency
		ldx sid_registers + $12
		inx 
		stx voice3_control
; jumped to by: voice_3_tick
lC3B0:
		jsr voice_3_read_parameter
		asl 
		sta voice_3_duration
		jmp lC323
		.byte	$00

; called by: voice_3_tick, start_voice_3
; jumped to by: dC3CD
voice_3_set_page:
		lda voice_3_page
		clc 
		adc #$b0
		sta $c3dd
		adc #$08
		sta dC3E4
		rts 

; called by: voice_3_tick
; written by: start_voice_3
voice_3_next:
		inc voice_3_position
; written by: start_voice_3
dC3CD:
		ldy voice_3_position
		bne lC3D7
		inc voice_3_page
		bne voice_3_set_page
; jumped to by: dC3CD
lC3D7:
		rts 

; called by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_read_note:
		ldy voice_3_position
		lda voice_3_notes, y
		rts 

; called by: voice_3_tick
voice_3_read_parameter:
		ldy voice_3_position
		lda voice_3_parameters, y
; written by: voice_3_set_page
dC3E4 = $c3e4
		rts 

; jumped to by: voice_3_tick, silence_voice_3
voice_3_silence:
		lda voices_playing
		and #$fb
		sta voices_playing
		lda #$00
		sta voice_3_in_call
		sta voice_3_repeat_count
		sta voice_3_duration
		sta voice_3_speed_countdown
		sta voice_3_position
		rts 

; the music player's API; only $c4d0 and $c4fb are called from the game
; jumped to by: unpack_music
copy_music_data:
		lda #$90
		sta lC40C + 2
		lda #$e0
		sta dC411
; jumped to by: copy_music_data
lC40A:
		ldy #$00
; jumped to by: copy_music_data
; written by: copy_music_data
; modified by: copy_music_data
lC40C:
		lda voice_2_notes, y
		sta untouched_f000, y
; read by: copy_music_data
; written by: copy_music_data
; modified by: copy_music_data
dC411 = $c411
		iny 
		bne lC40C
		inc lC40C + 2
		inc dC411
		lda dC411
		cmp #$f0
		bne lC40A
		rts 

; called by: play_title_music, play_game_music, music_init
init_sid:
		ldx #$18
; jumped to by: init_sid
lC425:
		lda sid_registers, x
		sta voice1_frequency, x
		dex 
		bne lC425
		rts 

; called by: play_game_music, set_music, music_init
install_irq:
		sei 
		lda #<irq
		sta irq_vector
		lda #>irq
		sta irq_vector + $1
		cli 
		rts 

; starts a voice at its position: patches voice_n_next to not advance, ticks once, unpatches
; called by: music_play_1, music_play_1_2, music_play_1_3, music_play_all
start_voice_1:
		lda #$ac
		sta voice_1_next
		lda #$60
		sta dC1CD
		jsr voice_1_set_page
		jsr voice_1_tick
		lda #$ee
		sta voice_1_next
		lda #$ac
		sta dC1CD
		rts 

; called by: music_play_2, music_play_1_2, music_play_2_3, music_play_all
start_voice_2:
		lda #$ac
		sta voice_2_next
		lda #$60
		sta dC2CD
		jsr voice_2_set_page
		jsr voice_2_tick
		lda #$ee
		sta voice_2_next
		lda #$ac
		sta dC2CD
		rts 

; called by: music_play_3, music_play_1_3, music_play_2_3, music_play_all
start_voice_3:
		lda #$ac
		sta voice_3_next
		lda #$60
		sta dC3CD
		jsr voice_3_set_page
		jsr voice_3_tick
		lda #$ee
		sta voice_3_next
		lda #$ac
		sta dC3CD
		rts 

; switch the KERNAL ROM out and in again (the game runs with $01 = $37)
; called by: music_play_1, music_play_2, music_play_3, music_play_1_2, music_play_1_3, music_play_2_3, music_play_all, irq
; jumped to by: bank_all_ram
music_bank_out:
		lda cpu_port
		and #$fd
		sta cpu_port
		rts 

; called by: music_play_1, music_play_2, music_play_3, music_play_1_2, music_play_1_3, music_play_2_3, music_play_all, snapshot_pc, unpack_music
music_bank_in:
		lda cpu_port
		ora #$03
		sta cpu_port
		rts 

; puts the KERNAL's IRQ back
music_stop:
		sei 
		lda #>kernal_irq
		sta irq_vector + $1
		lda #<kernal_irq
		sta irq_vector
		cli 
		rts 

; start voices, and mark them in voices_playing
music_play_1:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_1
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$01
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

music_play_2:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_2
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$02
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

music_play_3:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_3
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$04
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

music_play_1_2:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_1
		jsr start_voice_2
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$03
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

; called by: next_player, play_title_music, play_game_music
music_play_1_3:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_1
		jsr start_voice_3
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$05
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

music_play_2_3:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_2
		jsr start_voice_3
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$06
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

music_play_all:
		sei 
		jsr music_bank_out
		jsr start_voice_1
		jsr start_voice_2
		jsr start_voice_3
		jsr music_bank_in
		lda voices_playing
		ora #$07
		sta voices_playing
		cli 
		rts 

; plays the voices in voices_playing, then goes on to the KERNAL's IRQ
irq:
		jsr music_bank_out
		lda voices_playing
		beq lC566
		lsr 
		pha 
; where the CPU was when the snapshot was taken
snapshot_pc:
		bcc lC555
		jsr voice_1_play
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
; jumped to by: snapshot_pc
lC555:
		pla 
		lsr 
		pha 
		bcc lC55D
		jsr voice_2_play
; jumped to by: snapshot_pc
lC55D:
		pla 
		lsr 
		bcc lC566
		nop 
		nop 
		jsr voice_3_play
; jumped to by: irq, snapshot_pc
lC566:
		jsr music_bank_in
		jmp kernal_irq

; called by: music_silence_1, music_silence_1_2, music_silence_1_3, music_silence_all
silence_voice_1:
		lda #$00
		sta voice1_control
		sta voice1_attack_decay
		jmp voice_1_silence

; called by: music_silence_2, music_silence_1_2, music_silence_2_3, music_silence_all
silence_voice_2:
		lda #$00
		sta voice2_frequency
		sta voice2_frequency + $1
		jmp voice_2_silence

; called by: music_silence_3, music_silence_1_3, music_silence_2_3, music_silence_all
silence_voice_3:
		lda #$00
		sta voice3_frequency
		sta voice3_frequency + $1
		jmp voice_3_silence

music_silence_1:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_1
		cli 
		rts 

music_silence_2:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_2
		cli 
		rts 

music_silence_3:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_3
		cli 
		rts 

music_silence_1_2:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_1
		jsr silence_voice_2
		cli 
		rts 

; called by: lose_life, touch_exit, play_title_music, play_game_music
music_silence_1_3:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_1
		jsr silence_voice_3
		cli 
		rts 

music_silence_2_3:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_2
		jsr silence_voice_3
		cli 
		rts 

music_silence_all:
		sei 
		jsr silence_voice_1
		jsr silence_voice_2
		jsr silence_voice_3
		cli 
		rts 

; the set-up, run once by the loader: unpacks the music, then the SID and the IRQ
music_init:
		jsr unpack_music
		jsr init_sid
		jsr install_irq
		rts 

; RAM everywhere
bank_all_ram:
		lda cpu_port
		and #$fc
		sta cpu_port
		rts 

		lda cpu_port
		ora #$02
		sta cpu_port
		jmp music_bank_out

; spreads six blocks of the loaded music over the stream pages, clears the rest of each page,
; and copies voice 1's to $e000
; called by: music_init
unpack_music:
		sei 
		jsr lC7EB
		jsr move_blocks
		jsr clear_block_tails
		jsr music_bank_in
		cli 
		ldx #$04
; jumped to by: unpack_music
lC5F0:
		lda $c5fb, x
		sta dBFFF, x
		dex 
		bne lC5F0
		jmp copy_music_data

; the size ($0502: 2 bytes, 5 pages) and source ($2000) of each block
unpack_parameters:
		.byte	$02, $05, $00, $20

; called by: unpack_music
move_blocks:
		ldx #$04
; jumped to by: move_blocks
lC602:
		lda dBFFF, x
		sta $5f, x
		dex 
		bne lC602
		lda #$80
; jumped to by: lC746
lC60C:
		sta lC613 + 1
		ldx #$00
		stx $6d
; jumped to by: move_blocks
; written by: move_blocks
lC613:
		lda block_pages + $1, x
		sta $65
		lda #$00
		sta $64
		jsr move_block
		jsr next_block_source
		inc $6d
		ldx $6d
		cpx #$06
		bne lC613
		rts 

; called by: move_blocks
next_block_source:
		lda $62
		clc 
		adc $60
		sta $62
		bcc lC636
		inc $63
; jumped to by: next_block_source
lC636:
		tax 
		inx 
		bne lC63C
		inc $63
; jumped to by: next_block_source
lC63C:
		stx $62
		rts 

; called by: move_blocks
move_block:
		ldx $61
		beq lC64D
; jumped to by: move_block
lC643:
		jsr copy_page
		inc $63
		inc $65
		dex 
		bne lC643
; jumped to by: move_block
lC64D:
		ldy $60
		beq lC657
		jsr copy_bytes
		jsr lC664
; jumped to by: move_block
lC657:
		rts 

; called by: lC719
copy_page_down:
		ldy #$00
; called by: lC719
lC65A:
		lda ($62), y
		sta ($64), y
		dey 
		bne lC65A
		rts 

; called by: lC719
; jumped to by: lC719
copy_byte:
		ldy #$00
; called by: move_block
lC664:
		lda ($62), y
		sta ($64), y
		rts 

; called by: move_block
copy_page:
		ldy #$00
; jumped to by: copy_page
lC66B:
		lda ($62), y
		sta ($64), y
		iny 
		bne lC66B
		rts 

; called by: move_block
copy_bytes:
		ldy #$00
; jumped to by: copy_bytes
lC675:
		lda ($62), y
		sta ($64), y
		iny 
		cpy $60
		bne lC675
		rts 

; the pages blocks go to: $9000-$b800, and $4000-$7000 for the variant at $c750
; read by: move_blocks, clear_block_tails, lC6CE
block_pages:
		.byte	$00, $90, $98, $a0, $a8, $b0, $b8, $c0, $00, $40, $48, $50, $58, $60, $68, $70
		.byte	$00

; called by: unpack_music
clear_block_tails:
		lda #$80
		sta dC6AE
		lda #$90
; jumped to by: lC6CE
lC697:
		sta $63
		lda #$00
		sta $62
		ldx #$01
; jumped to by: clear_block_tails
lC69F:
		ldy $60
		jsr lC6BE
		nop 
		nop 
		jsr lC6CE
; jumped to by: clear_block_tails
lC6A9:
		inc $63
		lda $63
		cmp block_pages + $1, x
; written by: clear_block_tails, lC6CE
dC6AE = $c6ae
		beq lC6B8
		jsr lC6CC
		clc 
		bcc lC6A9
; jumped to by: clear_block_tails
lC6B8:
		inx 
		cpx #$07
		bne lC69F
		rts 

; called by: clear_block_tails
lC6BE:
		lda $63
		clc 
		adc $61
		sta $63
		iny 
		bne lC6CB
		inc $63
		nop 
; jumped to by: lC6BE
lC6CB:
		rts 

; called by: clear_block_tails
lC6CC:
		ldy #$00
; called by: clear_block_tails
lC6CE:
		lda #$00
; jumped to by: lC6CE
lC6D0:
		sta ($62), y
		iny 
		bne lC6D0
		rts 

		lda #$88
		sta dC6AE
		lda #$40
		jmp lC697

		lda #$87
		sta dC703
		lda #$6f
		sta $65
		bne lC6F4
		lda #$7f
		sta dC703
		lda #$bf
		sta $65
; jumped to by: lC6CE
lC6F4:
		lda #$06
		sta $6d
		lda #$ff
		sta $64
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
		nop 
; jumped to by: lC6CE
lC700:
		ldx $6d
		lda block_pages + $8, x
; written by: lC6CE
dC703 = $c703
		sta $63
		lda #$00
		sta $62
		jsr lC719
		dec $6d
		beq lC718
		jsr lC746
		clc 
		bcc lC700
; jumped to by: lC6CE
lC718:
		rts 

; called by: lC6CE
lC719:
		lda $63
		clc 
		adc $61
		sta $63
		lda $64
		sec 
		sbc $60
		sta $64
		bcs lC72B
		dec $65
; jumped to by: lC719
lC72B:
		ldy $60
		beq lC732
		jsr lC65A
; jumped to by: lC719
lC732:
		jsr copy_byte
		ldx $61
		beq lC743
; jumped to by: lC719
lC739:
		dec $63
		dec $65
		jsr copy_page_down
		dex 
		bne lC739
; jumped to by: lC719
lC743:
		jmp copy_byte

; called by: lC6CE
lC746:
		ldx $64
		bne lC74C
		dec $65
; jumped to by: lC746
lC74C:
		dex 
		stx $64
		rts 

		ldx #$04
; jumped to by: lC746
lC752:
		lda unused_6fbe + $41, x
		sta $5f, x
		dex 
		bne lC752
		lda #$88
		jmp lC60C

		ldy #$ff
		ldx $66
		jsr setlfs
		lda $6a
		ldx #$00
		ldy #$cf
		jsr setnam
		rts 

; leftover of the loader

		.byte	$a2, $04, $a9, $64, $20, $d8, $ff, $4c, $d7, $3a, $20, $00, $3b, $a2, $10, $bd
		.byte	$af, $02, $9d, $ff, $ce, $ca, $d0, $f7, $4c, $b7, $c7, $ea, $ea, $a5, $60, $8d
		.byte	$00, $70, $a5, $61, $8d, $01, $70, $a5, $64, $8d, $02, $70, $a5, $65, $8d, $03
		.byte	$70, $60, $a5, $60, $8d, $00, $c0, $a5, $61, $8d, $01, $c0, $a5, $64, $8d, $02
		.byte	$c0, $a5, $65, $8d, $03, $c0, $60, $a6, $67, $e8, $86, $6a, $60, $20, $d7, $3a
		.byte	$a2, $0f, $bd, $d3, $c7, $9d, $21, $8f, $ca, $d0, $f7, $a5, $c5, $c5, $c5, $f0
		.byte	$fa, $4c, $90, $3b, $0c, $0f, $01, $04, $20, $05, $12, $12, $0f, $12, $20, $20
		.byte	$20, $20, $20, $20, $00, $20, $b7, $ff, $4c, $d1, $3b

; called by: unpack_music
lC7EB:
		lda cpu_port
		and #$fe
		sta cpu_port
		rts 

; more of the loader, then a copy of the KERNAL's $fd00 page, repeated

		.byte	$00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $00, $20, $13, $30, $4c, $70, $c7, $a2
		.byte	$00, $bd, $00, $78, $9d, $00, $c8, $ca, $d0, $f7, $ee, $05, $78, $ee, $08, $78
		.byte	$ad, $05, $78, $c9, $7a, $d0, $e8, $4c, $e0, $c5, $a0, $7a, $a2, $90, $a9, $40
		.byte	$8d, $2f, $c8, $8e, $32, $c8, $8c, $40, $c8, $a2, $00, $bd, $00, $c8, $9d, $00
		.byte	$78, $ca, $d0, $f7, $ee, $2f, $c8, $ee, $32, $c8, $ad, $2f, $c8, $c9, $ca, $d0
		.byte	$e8, $60, $78, $20, $d0, $c5, $a9, $40, $a2, $d0, $a0, $70, $20, $22, $c8, $a9
		.byte	$90, $a2, $40, $a0, $ca, $20, $22, $c8, $20, $94, $c4, $58, $60, $78, $20, $d0
		.byte	$c5, $a9, $40, $a2, $d0, $a0, $70, $20, $22, $c8, $20, $94, $c4, $58, $60, $78
		.byte	$20, $d0, $c5, $a9, $d0, $a2, $40, $a0, $00, $20, $22, $c8, $20, $94, $c4, $58
